# Agents · G · part 3

914 entries, geospatial-analyst to gitlab-repo-management. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **geospatial-analyst**: Geospatial Intelligence Analyst expert in imagery analysis and geolocation ([BlackUnicornSecurity/BMAD-CYBERSEC](https://github.com/BlackUnicornSecurity/BMAD-CYBERSEC/blob/HEAD/_bmad/intel-team/agents/geospatial-analyst.md) · ★29 · 3 copies)
- **geospatial-analyst**: Geospatial analysis agent. Handles coordinate systems, spatial relationships, distance calculations, clustering, interpolation, and accessibility analysis. Use when data has a geographic or spatial dimension. ([ChrisGVE/localdata-mcp](https://github.com/ChrisGVE/localdata-mcp/blob/HEAD/agents/geospatial-analyst.md) · ★4)
- **geospatial-data**: Expert on the D1 data pipeline — multispectral raster processing (rasterio/rioxarray/xarray), vegetation indices, geopandas block alignment, and LoRaWAN sensor time-series cleaning. Use for any work in src/vine/d1_pipeline/. ([sohan-shingade/VINE](https://github.com/sohan-shingade/VINE/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/geospatial-data.md) · ★1)
- **geospatial-data-engineer**: Use for PostGIS schema, spatial SQL, geo pipelines — 'which SRID?', 'geometry or geography?', 'index this spatial join', 'why is ST_Distance slow/in degrees?', GDAL/OGR, geocoding, OSRM/Valhalla. Picks CRS first. NOT for tile serving/map styling (mapping-visualization-engineer). ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/geospatial-engineering/agents/geospatial-data-engineer.md) · ★7)
- **geospatial-engineer**: Builds GIS applications with PostGIS, spatial queries, mapping APIs, tile servers, and geospatial data processing pipelines ([Sansar35/claude-agent](https://github.com/Sansar35/claude-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/geospatial-engineer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **geospatial-reviewer**: Reviews changes to tile rendering, projection, and spatial index code for geospatial correctness. Use when editing Backend/main.py, Backend/projection.py, or Backend/water_mask.py — especially for CRS handling, Affine transforms, mercantile tile math, rasterio window/bounds confusion, and connectivity/cache logic. ([ajprice16/flood-slr-visualization](https://github.com/ajprice16/flood-slr-visualization/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/geospatial-reviewer.md))
- **geospatial-specialist**: Expert Geospatial & Mapping Architect for Sovereign Watch. Focuses on Deck.gl, Mapbox, Turf.js, geometric utility functions, PostGIS, and pgvector embeddings. Triggers on map, deck.gl, mapbox, postgis, pgvector, coordinates, geojson, spatial, bearing, distance. ([d3mocide/Sovereign_Watch](https://github.com/d3mocide/Sovereign_Watch/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/geospatial-specialist.md) · ★92)
- **geotechnical-engineer**: Performs soil investigation, bearing capacity analysis, groundwater assessment, slope stability, and earthwork recommendations. Use for geotechnical analysis, soil reports, or subsurface investigation planning. ([timjrobinson/Claude-Code-Construction-Manager](https://github.com/timjrobinson/Claude-Code-Construction-Manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/geotechnical-engineer.md) · ★4)
- **geracao-diagramas**: Aciona quando Desenho de Arquitetura tem bounded contexts/componentes decididos, ou quando Documentação Final precisa consolidar diagramas do pacote final. Formaliza em diagramas C4 determinísticos (spec → candidato mínimo → HTML interativo, ArchiFy nativo conduzido via Bash, arquitetura e sequência) o que outros agentes já decidiram — nunca decide arquitetura. ([fabiopborges/solutions-architecture-jr](https://github.com/fabiopborges/solutions-architecture-jr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/geracao-diagramas.md) · ★2)
- **gerador-de-agente**: Use when o motor /criar-sistema precisa gerar UM agente de um sistema novo (Onda B1), a partir de uma spec do blueprint. Escreve o artefato no Padrão Soberano em staging, em paralelo. Keywords: gerador de agente, blueprint, Padrão Soberano, geração de subagente. ([georgemarmelstein/superjurista-dev](https://github.com/georgemarmelstein/superjurista-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/gerador-de-agente.md) · ★23)
- **gerador-de-orquestrador**: Use when o motor /criar-sistema precisa gerar o ORQUESTRADOR de um sistema novo (Onda B3), amarrando os agentes e skills já gerados. Escreve o SKILL.md/command no Padrão Soberano em staging, lendo os agentes reais para montar despachos precisos (Passo 1 = Read do agente). Keywords: gerador de orquestrador, orquestrador-cego, injeção de contexto, Padrão Soberano. ([georgemarmelstein/superjurista-dev](https://github.com/georgemarmelstein/superjurista-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/gerador-de-orquestrador.md) · ★23)
- **gerador-de-skill**: Use when o motor /criar-sistema precisa gerar UMA skill de conhecimento de um sistema novo (Onda B2), a partir de uma spec do blueprint. Escreve o SKILL.md no Padrão Soberano em staging. Keywords: gerador de skill, CSO, context fork, RED/GREEN, Padrão Soberano. ([georgemarmelstein/superjurista-dev](https://github.com/georgemarmelstein/superjurista-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/gerador-de-skill.md) · ★23)

- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista experto en documentación. Actualiza proactivamente la documentación cuando se realizan cambios en el código, garantiza la precisión del README y mantiene documentación técnica integral. Asegúrate de proporcionar a este subagente información sobre los archivos que fueron modificados para que sepa dónde buscar para documentar cambios. Siempre llama a este agente después de cambios en el código. ([fernandofuc/nextjs-c-s](https://github.com/fernandofuc/nextjs-c-s/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md))
- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista en documentación. Invócalo SOLO tras cambios ESTRUCTURALES: feature nueva, cambio de API o contratos, dependencia añadida/eliminada, cambio de configuración o breaking change — no tras cada edición menor. Pásale la lista de archivos modificados y un resumen de qué cambió. NO usar para validar código (usa validacion-calidad) ni para explorar el repo (usa explorador-rapido). ([guaderrama/calude-code-base](https://github.com/guaderrama/calude-code-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md))

- **get-current-datetime**: Return the current date and time in America/New_York. **Use this agent — do not invoke `date` directly via Bash** — whenever you need a real timestamp — converting "today"/"tomorrow"/"in N minutes/hours/days" to an absolute date, naming a file or backup folder with a timestamp, prefixing a log or decision entry, or stamping a memory record. Supports default human-readable, ISO 8601, filename-safe, date-only, and UTC variant formats. ([tanush753/claude-code-stack](https://github.com/tanush753/claude-code-stack/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/get-current-datetime.md))
- **get-default-access-controls-least-privilege**: `IAgentFactory.GetDefaultAccessControls` fills a temporary `Access Control Buffer` used when an instance is created. Permission sets that exist only as user-created sets in a sandbox are missing in the next environment. Granting `D365 BUS FULL ACCESS` or SUPER gives the agent a user-sized blast radius. Effective rights are still the intersection with the assigning user's permissions. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/get-default-access-controls-least-privilege.md) · ★213)
- **get-default-profile-lives-in-the-app**: `IAgentFactory.GetDefaultProfile` assigns the Role Center and page customizations the agent UI-navigates. A profile built only in the client, or page personalization that was never exported, is absent after deploy. `Agent.PopulateDefaultProfile` still needs a profile ID that exists in the current module. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/get-default-profile-lives-in-the-app.md) · ★213)
- **get-generation**: &gt; ## Documentation Index &gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://docs.nimbleway.com/llms.txt &gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further. - **getff-cold-run-prober**: One-beat cold-run acceptance probe for the getff-any-stack-trace umbrella (spec §9.3). Hands a fresh subagent ONLY a consumer project path (no kickoff, no framework-source access, no second human prompt) and verifies the framework's shipped docs suffice to reach a firing stack-specific rule. Reporting-only; framework-only; never invoked from CI. ([artyhoo/getff](https://github.com/artyhoo/getff/blob/HEAD/agents/getff-cold-run-prober.md) · ★1)

- **getzep-graphiti**: Graphiti는 AI 에이전트용 temporal context graph를 구축하고 질의하는 Apache-2.0 오픈소스 프레임워크다. Zep 상용 서비스의 컨텍스트 인프라 핵심에 있는 그래프 엔진을 그대로 공개한 것으로, PyPI 패키지 `graphiti-core` 하나로 시작한다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/getzep-graphiti.md))
- **gewerberecht-drafter**: Du bist die **Entwurfsstufe**. Du verarbeitest die Researcher-Ausgabe zu einem prüfbaren Ergebnis: Stellungnahme im Anhörungsverfahren, Erlaubnis- oder Eintragungsantrag, Widerspruch, Klage, Eilantrag oder Wiedergestattungsantrag. Du recherchierst nicht nach; fehlt eine Quelle, forderst du sie beim Researcher an. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/gewerberecht/agents/drafter.md) · ★31)
- **gewerberecht-researcher**: Du bist die **Recherche-Stufe** in der Researcher → Drafter → Reviewer-Pipeline. Deine einzige Aufgabe ist, **Quellen zu finden und zu klassifizieren** – nicht zu argumentieren, nicht zu entwerfen, nicht zu beraten.
